- **visual-debug-reviewer**: Phase E LLM visual review for visual-debug — read ref/impl PNG pairs at every scroll position, judge PASS / PARTIAL / FAIL semantically, return a verdict table. Reads the protocol from skills/visual-debug/comparison-fix.md Phase E section. Explicit `model: opus` guarantees consistent quality regardless of parent-agent model. - **Vitor Visão**: 1. **Fórmula 6 elementos é inegociável** — Subject → Action → Environment → Camera → Style → Constraints.
